## Users parity provenance

Local DuckDB: 51 green / 4 retire_verified / 0 red (retirement evidence is not green-migrated).
Snowflake: **red** — role SFX_D1110_ROLE not authorized on SFX_1119 (002003); live capture/prove not run.
These are separate evidence legs; local green does not imply live Snowflake parity.

## Documents parity provenance

Local DuckDB: **50 green / 4 approved deviation / 1 amber / 0 red / 4 retire candidates**, 59 total.
Snowflake: **red/denied** — role SFX_D1110_ROLE not authorized on SFX_1119 (002003); live capture/prove not run.
Retire candidates are excluded from executable parity, not approved for deletion. Relation counts below sum within each proof leg; relations can repeat across legs.

### Owner-decision comparisons

`approved_deviation` is not raw parity or pending confirmation. Every differing row must be in the independent decision scope and the complete converted result must match the approved expectation. Raw hashes and every differing row remain in the evidence. Unexplained differences remain red. Storage-derived deviations require amber and finance confirmation.

Users/content DISPLAY_NAME is VARCHAR(200), checked against full input with raw legacy truncation retained (100 of 120 names). Audit summary IDs use stable date/key ordering with raw legacy IDs retained.

- SELECT DISTINCT drops exact duplicates to match Teradata SET semantics (300 to 250). Billing and audit consume the deduped core table. (approved-pending-confirmation). Finance still has to confirm the invoice impact.
- Owner-approved: highest VERSION_NO wins tied latest VERSION_TS. Deliberately differs from legacy arbitrary selection. (approved).
- Owner-approved: highest privilege wins, ADMIN then EDITOR then other roles; no role leaves the source access unchanged. Deliberately differs from legacy first cached match. (approved).

## DBC parity provenance

Local ACCOUNT_USAGE shapes are assumed from identical DBQL CSVs; live ACCOUNT_USAGE is unverified (role lacks grant; no connection attempted). Owner approved unavailable CPU, IO and client-id fields; all other differences stay red. This is not catalog deletion approval.
Mapped-columns ladders exclude the named decision columns **and explicitly labelled unsupported row kinds**; their green result is not full-shape parity.

| Object | local_duckdb / snowflake | Full rows old/new; hash delta | Mapped rows old/new; hash delta | Retirement reads | Exact source / replacement / evidence / ladder |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
